# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 1,
  "instanceId": "example",
  "role": "example"
}
```

## AccountAvailability

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAvailability.json), input validation.

Type: ["available","near_limit","exhausted","logged_out","unknown"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"unknown"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountEnvKey.json), input validation.

Type: ["CODEX_HOME","CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR","XDG_DATA_HOME","XDG_CONFIG_HOME","XDG_STATE_HOME","XDG_CACHE_HOME","CURSOR_DATA_DIR","PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "logged_out",
  "blockers": {},
  "observedAt": 6,
  "usage": {
    "costUsd": 2
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "acpAgentId": "example",
  "availability": "logged_out",
  "homeStrategy": "default_cli",
  "id": "example",
  "installationId": "example",
  "isDefault": true,
  "label": "example",
  "provider": "opencode",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "unknown",
    "observedAt": 4,
    "usage": {
      "costUsd": 5,
      "outputTokens": 8
    },
    "windows": {}
  }
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |
| raw | yes | object | {"properties":{"json":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["json","truncated"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "imageInput": true,
    "planMode": true,
    "resume": true
  },
  "coverage": "generic",
  "mcp": "stdio",
  "modeSelection": false,
  "modelSelection": false,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": true
  },
  "subagentSessions": true,
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

| parentId | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| origin | yes | [AgentOrigin.json](schema/AgentOrigin.json) |  |
| lineage | no | [ThreadLineage.json](schema/ThreadLineage.json) |  |
| native | yes | [NativeRef.json](schema/NativeRef.json) |  |
| fidelity | yes | [AgentFidelity.json](schema/AgentFidelity.json) |  |
| spawnedBy | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ItemId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| name | no | string |  |
| role | no | string |  |
| model | no | string |  |
| cwd | yes | string |  |
| status | yes | [AgentStatus.json](schema/AgentStatus.json) |  |
| background | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

  "createdAt": 8,
  "cwd": "example",
  "endedAt": 9,
  "fidelity": "full",
  "id": "example",
  "lineage": {
    "lossy": false,
    "mode": "portable",
    "parentAgentId": "example",
    "parentThreadId": "example",
    "point": {

      "type": "turn"
    }
  },
  "model": "example",
  "native": {
    "acpAgentId": "example",
    "aliases": [],
    "forkedFromNativeId": "example",
    "instanceId": "example",
    "nativeId": "example",
    "provider": "cursor"
  },
  "origin": "provider_subagent",
  "parentId": "example",
  "status": {
    "activity": "tool",
    "state": "working"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}

## AgentControlOperation

[JSON Schema](schema/AgentControlOperation.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"delegate_task"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":16384} |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| permissionMode | no | [PermissionMode.json](schema/PermissionMode.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| options | no | [AgentLaunchOptions.json](schema/AgentLaunchOptions.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "acpAgentId": "example",
  "op": "delegate_task",
  "provider": "claude",
  "requestId": "FX",
  "role": "example",
  "task": "example"
}
```

### Variant 2

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| complete | yes | boolean |  |

| parts | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ContentPart.json"}} |
| attachments | no | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Attachment.json"}} |
| synthetic | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| origin | no | [MessageOrigin.json](schema/MessageOrigin.json) |  |
| notAnswered | no | `"stopped"` |  |
| mergedContext | no | [MergedForkContext.json](schema/MergedForkContext.json) |  |

  "parts": [],
  "role": "assistant",
  "type": "message"
}

## CommandsList

[JSON Schema](schema/CommandsList.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one of threadId or draft is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |

| type | yes | `"commands.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":1,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "draft": {
    "draftId": "example",
    "provider": "pi",
    "workspaceId": "example"
  },
  "limit": 3,
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "commands.list"
}
```

### events

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

| type | yes | `"events"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |

| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| events | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeliveryEvent.json"}} |

  "events": [],
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 7,
  "type": "events"
}
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"cursor.auth.login"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| loginId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| instanceId | yes | [AccountId.json](schema/AccountId.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["starting","browser","complete","failed","cancelled"] |  |
| expiresAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| url | no | intersection | {"allOf":[{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":8192,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."},{"type":"string","pattern":"^(?!.*\\s)https:\\/\\/\\S+$"}]} |
| auth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| error | no | ["login_failed","expired","cancelled"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "expiresAt": 5,
  "instanceId": "PXRQB3BWpQK7faSPSrnUVqpeJSG5UIWi5wg_YPsf02MHydCTjiq-rQCrzMTpE3Y8mrAIAxc4Ze5eJug5jf6mlwLFbareMX0V",
  "loginId": "example",
  "requestId": "example",
  "state": "failed",
  "type": "cursor.auth.login"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/DiagnosticsHealth.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| at | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| eventLoop | yes | object | {"properties":{"meanMs":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]},"p99Ms":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]},"maxMs":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["meanMs","p99Ms","maxMs"]} |
| memory | yes | object | {"properties":{"rssBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"heapUsedBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"heapTotalBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["rssBytes","heapUsedBytes","heapTotalBytes"]} |
| openHandles | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| sqlite | yes | object | {"properties":{"pageBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]},"walBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["pageBytes","walBytes"]} |
| activeSessions | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| queues | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"maxProperties":64,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 64 queue entries."} |
| logs | yes | object | {"properties":{"dropped":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"failed":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"queued":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"directory":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}},"required":["dropped","failed","queued"]} |

Example:

  "activeSessions": 6,
  "at": 0,
  "eventLoop": {
    "maxMs": 1,
    "meanMs": 8,

  },
  "logs": {

    "queued": 7
  },

    "heapUsedBytes": 9,
    "rssBytes": 9
  },
  "openHandles": 9,
  "queues": {},
  "sqlite": {
    "pageBytes": 6,
    "walBytes": 8
  }
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| path | yes | string |  |
| kind | yes | ["add","update","delete","move"] |  |
| movePath | no | string |  |
| diff | no | string |  |
| oldText | no | string,null |  |
| newText | no | string |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "update",
  "newText": "example",
  "path": "example"
}
```

### files.abort

### files.pull

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"files.pull"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| channel | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":4294967295} |

Example:

  "channel": 3,
  "requestId": "example",

}

### files.chunk

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"files.chunk"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| channel | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":4294967295} |
| offset | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| data | yes | string | {"minLength":4,"maxLength":87384,"pattern":"^(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{4})*(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{2}==\|[A-Za-z0-9+/]{3}=)?$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "channel": 2,
  "data": "ujQWq+9d90kpMpbz413pNOZEOBtQ8k==",
  "offset": 1,
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "files.chunk"
}
```

### files.request

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"files.request"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| operation | yes | [FileOperation.json](schema/FileOperation.json) |  |

Example:

  "operation": {
    "expected": null,

    "path": "example",
    "text": "example"
  },
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "files.request"
}
```

### files.credit

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"files.credit"` |  |
| channel | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":4294967295} |
| credits | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |

Example:

```json

  "channel": 10,
  "credits": 2,
  "type": "files.credit"
}
```

### files.cancel

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"files.cancel"` |  |
| channel | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":4294967295} |

| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Repository-relative path without NUL bytes."} |
| from | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Repository-relative path without NUL bytes."} |
| status | yes | ["added","modified","deleted","renamed","untracked"] |  |
| additions | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| deletions | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| binary | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Example:

```json
{
  "additions": 2,
  "deletions": 7,
  "from": "example",
  "path": "example",
  "status": "modified"
}
```

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |

Example:

```json
{
  "aroundSeq": 3,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

## ItemsWindowResponse

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowResponse.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| targetSeq | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Item.json"}} |
| itemSeqs | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}} |
| itemsBefore | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| itemsAfter | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "itemSeqs": {},
  "items": [],
  "itemsAfter": 3,
  "itemsBefore": null,
  "requestId": "example",
  "seq": 7,
  "targetSeq": null,
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

| status | yes | [NotificationStatus.json](schema/NotificationStatus.json) |  |
| interactionId | no | intersection | {"allOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionId.json"},{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":200}]} |
| backgroundCount | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":1000000} |
| actions | yes | array | {"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"action":{"type":"string","enum":["approve","deny"]},"optionId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":200}},"required":["action","optionId"]}} |
| preview | no | string | {"maxLength":300} |

Example:

```json
{
  "actions": [],
  "backgroundCount": 4,
  "id": "example",
  "status": "done",
  "threadId": "example",
  "title": "example"
}
```

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff"},"chunkTool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff_chunk","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff_chunk"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

Example:

```json

  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 9,
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 3,
  "policy": "recent-complete-items",
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 8,
  "version": 1
}
```

## PreviewLaunch

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewLaunch.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Choose exactly one executable, or an attach URL without an executable or autoPort.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| command | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| runtimeExecutable | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| args | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096}} |
| cwd | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| env | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| port | no | [PreviewPort.json](schema/PreviewPort.json) |  |
| autoPort | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

Example:

```json
{
  "command": "node",
  "name": "example"
}
```

## ProjectCloneUrl

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectCloneUrl.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: GitHub owner/repo shorthand, HTTPS, SSH or scp-style git@ URL with a host and repository path; no passwords, HTTPS usernames, whitespace or control bytes.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"ssh://git@example.invalid/repo.git"
```

## ProjectCommand

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectCommand.json), input validation.

### workspace.add

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.add"` |  |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| name | no | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "path": "example",
  "type": "workspace.add"
}
```

### workspace.create

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.create"` |  |
| parent | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| name | yes | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |
| git | no | object | {"properties":{"initialBranch":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":1024}}} |
| gitignore | no | string | {"maxLength":65536} |

Example:

```json
{
  "gitignore": "example",
  "name": ",{'",
  "parent": "example",
  "type": "workspace.create"
}
```

### workspace.clone

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.clone"` |  |
| parent | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| name | yes | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |
| url | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "name": "'.z",
  "parent": "example",
  "type": "workspace.clone",
  "url": "example"
}
```

### workspace.rename

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.rename"` |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| name | yes | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "name": ")! y",
  "type": "workspace.rename",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

### workspace.remove

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.remove"` |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| archiveThreads | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Example:

```json
{
  "archiveThreads": false,
  "type": "workspace.remove",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

## ProjectDirectoryName

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectDirectoryName.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: A literal existing directory name; no forward slash or NUL. Host filesystem rules apply to backslashes.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"example"
```

## ProjectFolderMatch

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectFolderMatch.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | yes | [ProjectDirectoryName.json](schema/ProjectDirectoryName.json) |  |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| isGitRepo | yes | boolean |  |
| isProject | yes | boolean |  |
| lastOpened | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| recentScore | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| score | yes | number |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "isGitRepo": false,
  "isProject": true,
  "name": "example",
  "path": "example",
  "recentScore": 0,
  "score": 3
}
```

## ProjectGit

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectGit.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| root | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| branch | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","maxLength":1024},{"type":"null"}]} |
| defaultBranch | yes | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| remotes | yes | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"name":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"fetchUrls":{"maxItems":32,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}},"pushUrls":{"maxItems":32,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}}},"required":["name","fetchUrls","pushUrls"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "branch": null,
  "defaultBranch": "example",
  "remotes": [],
  "root": "example"
}
```

## ProjectInspection

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectInspection.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| git | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectGit.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| suggestedRepoRoot | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "git": {
    "branch": "example",
    "defaultBranch": "example",
    "remotes": [],
    "root": "example"
  },
  "path": "example",
  "suggestedRepoRoot": "example"
}
```

## ProjectName

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectName.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: One folder name, without separators, traversal or control characters.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"y}',(~}.|"
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectsRequest.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"projects.request"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| operation | yes | union | {"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"workspace.inspect"},"path":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}},"required":["op","path"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"fs.search"},"query":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"limit":{"default":30,"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":100},"showHidden":{"default":false,"type":"boolean"}},"required":["op","query"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"fs.complete"},"path":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"limit":{"default":50,"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":100},"showHidden":{"default":false,"type":"boolean"}},"required":["op","path"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"workspace.clone.validate"},"url":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096}},"required":["op","url"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"fs.home"}},"required":["op"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"fs.recentFolders"},"limit":{"default":20,"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":100}},"required":["op"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"fs.browse"},"path":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"after":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectDirectoryName.json"},"limit":{"default":50,"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":100},"showHidden":{"default":false,"type":"boolean"}},"required":["op","path"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"op":{"type":"string","const":"workspace.clone.cancel"},"commandId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/CommandId.json"}},"required":["op","commandId"]}]} |

Example:

```json

  "operation": {
    "limit": 6,
    "op": "fs.recentFolders"

  "requestId": "example",

{"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"path":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"git":{"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectGit.json"},{"type":"null"}]},"suggestedRepoRoot":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"kind":{"type":"string","const":"inspection"}},"required":["path","git","kind"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"search"},"query":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"indexing":{"type":"boolean"},"entries":{"maxItems":100,"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectFolderMatch.json"}},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["kind","query","indexing","entries","truncated"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"completion"},"path":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"candidates":{"maxItems":100,"type":"array","items":{"type":"object","properties":{"name":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectDirectoryName.json"},"path":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"isGitRepo":{"type":"boolean"},"isProject":{"type":"boolean"},"lastOpened":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"recentScore":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"score":{"type":"number"},"completion":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}},"required":["name","path","isGitRepo","isProject","recentScore","score","completion"]}},"commonPrefix":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["kind","path","candidates","commonPrefix","truncated"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"cloneUrl"},"url":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"name":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectDirectoryName.json"}},"required":["kind","url","name"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"kind":{"type":"string","const":"home"},"path":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"canonicalPath":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},"roots":{"maxItems":32,"type":"array","items":{"type":" ## PromptArgument

[JSON Schema](schema/PromptArgument.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: An argument default must have the declared type.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | ["string","number","boolean"] |  |
| required | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| default | no | [PromptValue.json](schema/PromptValue.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "type": "string"
}
```

## PromptArguments

[JSON Schema](schema/PromptArguments.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: At most 64 arguments.

Type: object. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
{}
```

## PromptValue

[JSON Schema](schema/PromptValue.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"example"
```

### Variant 2

Type: number. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
1
```

### Variant 3

Type: boolean. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
false
```

## ProviderConfiguration

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderConfiguration.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| instance | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"} |
| enabled | no | boolean |  |
| defaultModel | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$","x-ace-constraint":"The pseudo model ID default is not accepted; choose a concrete model ID."},{"type":"null"}]} |
| binaryPath | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"pattern":"^(?:\\/\|[A-Za-z]:[\\\\/]).*$","x-ace-constraint":"Absolute executable path with no control bytes."} |
| customModels | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"displayName":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"\\S"}},"required":["id","displayName"]},"x-ace-constraint":"Custom model IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| favourites | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| showOnlyFavourites | no | boolean |  |
| hideDeprecated | no | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "binaryPath": "M:/3X\\",
  "customModels": [],
  "enabled": true,
  "favourites": [],
  "hiddenGroups": [],
  "hideDeprecated": false,
  "provider": "pi",
  "shownModels": []
}
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "acpAgentId": "local:agent",
  "env": {},
  "homeDir": "/example/home",
  "homeStrategy": "default_cli",
  "id": "installed:default",
  "installationId": "installed",
  "instanceId": "installed:default",
  "label": "Local agent",
  "provider": "acp"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderKind.json), input validation.

Type: ["claude","codex","opencode","cursor","antigravity","acp","pi"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"pi"
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderStatus.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |

[JSON Schema](schema/PtyBytesSchema.json), input validation.

    "start": 1

  "state": "active"
}

| reply | no | [ReviewReply.json](schema/ReviewReply.json) |  |
| sessions | no | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ReviewSession.json"}} |
| comments | no | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ReviewComment.json"}} |
| replies | no | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ReviewReply.json"}} |
| nextCursor | no | string |  |
| intentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |

Example:

```json
{
  "comment": {
    "anchor": {
      "fingerprint": {
        "after": [],
        "before": [],
        "lines": [
          "example"
        ]
      },
      "position": {
        "end": 1,
        "file": "example.ts",
        "side": "new",
        "start": 1
      },
      "revision": {
        "kind": "working-tree"
      },
      "state": "active"
    },
    "id": "example",
    "originalAnchor": {
      "fingerprint": {
        "after": [],
        "before": [],
        "lines": [
          "example"
        ]
      },
      "position": {
        "end": 1,
        "file": "example.ts",
        "side": "new",
        "start": 1
      },
      "revision": {
        "kind": "working-tree"
      },
      "state": "active"
    },
    "resolved": true,
    "sessionId": "example",
    "text": "example"
  },
  "comments": [],
  "replies": [],
  "session": {
    "createdAt": 5,
    "id": "example",
    "source": {
      "from": {
        "kind": "working-tree"
      },
      "to": {
        "kind": "working-tree"
      },
      "workspaceId": "example"
    },
    "status": "open"
  }
}
```

## ReviewFingerprint

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Total UTF-16 length of before, lines and after is <= 8192.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| lines | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| after | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| noFinalNewline | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": [],
  "before": [],
  "lines": [
    "example"
  ],
  "noFinalNewline": true
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFixIntent.json), input validation.

  "sessionId": "example",

    "from": {
      "id": "example",
      "kind": "checkpoint"
    },
    "threadId": "example",
    "to": {
      "kind": "working-tree"
    },
    "workspaceId": "example",
    "worktree": "example"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}

## ReviewPath

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewPath.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Repository-relative path without control characters, backslashes, drive prefixes, empty, dot or parent segments.

## ReviewPosition

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewPosition.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: end >= start and end - start < 100.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| file | yes | [ReviewPath.json](schema/ReviewPath.json) |  |
| side | yes | ["old","new"] |  |
| start | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":10000000} |
| end | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":10000000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "end": 1,
  "file": "example.ts",
  "side": "new",
  "start": 1
}
```

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| background | no | boolean |  |
| maxSessions | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select","performSecondaryAction","selectText"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": true,
    "displays": true,
    "windows": false
  },
  "codecs": [
    "jpeg"

  "input": {
    "keyboard": true,
    "pointer": false,
    "scroll": false,
    "text": true
  },
  "permissions": {
    "input": "denied",
    "screen": "denied"
  },
  "platform": "linux-wayland",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": false,
  "version": 2
}
```

## ScreenFrameHeader

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenFrameHeader.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| sequence | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| timestamp | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| width | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":3840} |
| height | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":2160} |
| codec | yes | ["jpeg","h264"] |  |
| keyframe | no | boolean |  |
| videoCodec | no | string | {"pattern":"^avc1\\.[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$"} |
| scale | no | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0} |

| bytes | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":8388608} |

Example:

```json

  "bytes": 8,
  "codec": "jpeg",
  "height": 1,
  "sequence": 0,
  "sessionId": "x7UeO3T1FMpF1_2JEZllanEVydkN4BjV8nUY-rc-v1FQU4Voi2cu0Y5cWcSN",
  "timestamp": 7,
  "version": 1,
  "videoCodec": "avc1.cb5E8d",
  "width": 6
}

### Variant 2

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| captureGeneration | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| ts | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| width | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":3840} |
| height | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":2160} |
| scale | yes | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":8} |
| codec | yes | ["jpeg","h264"] |  |
| keyframe | no | boolean |  |
| videoCodec | no | string | {"pattern":"^avc1\\.[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$"} |
| dirtyRects | no | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenRect.json"}} |
| bytes | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":8388608} |

Example:

```json
{
  "bytes": 9,
  "codec": "h264",
  "height": 4,
  "scale": 4,
  "seq": 4,
  "sessionId": "CqUvl5Eh34IC7zY",
  "ts": 1,
  "version": 2,
  "width": 5
}

```

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenServerMessage.json), input validation.

### screen.enabled

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| enabled | yes | boolean |  |

  "type": "screen.enabled"
}

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"screen.state"` |  |
| state | yes | [ScreenState.json](schema/ScreenState.json) |  |

```json
{
  "state": {
    "controller": "none",

      "agentId": "example",
      "threadId": "example"
    },
    "indicator": false,
    "lifecycle": "stopping",
    "permissions": {
      "accessibility": true,
      "screenRecording": true
    },
    "sessionId": "e",
    "target": {
      "bundleId": "example",
      "displayId": 4,
      "kind": "app"
    }
  },
  "type": "screen.state"
}
```

### screen.result

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"screen.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| ok | yes | boolean |  |
| data | no | any JSON value |  |
| error | no | string |  |
| errorCode | no | ["forbidden","permission_denied","approval_required","screen_disabled","denied","read_only","target_gone","not_supported","bounds","busy","timeout","internal","target_busy","foreground_required","focus_changed","window_minimized","window_offscreen","secure_input_required","clipboard_changed"] |  |
| holder | no | object | {"properties":{"sessionId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenId.json"},"owner":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024}},"required":["sessionId","owner"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "holder": {
    "owner": "example",
    "sessionId": "rofXec9XeC2Pq7dqzg8v4ps8ntgKfkg9PQwgl2YbpaGly0aVNQdcuSH"
  },
  "ok": false,
  "requestId": "6ohhjNdpTvPTDaqhR",
  "type": "screen.result"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |

| controller | yes | ["agent","human","none"] |  |
| mode | no | [ScreenMode.json](schema/ScreenMode.json) | {"default":"background"} |
| secureInputAllowed | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

## SearchFilters

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchFilters.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: after must be <= before when both timestamps are present.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| after | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| before | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 8,
  "before": 9,
  "threadId": "example",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

  "highlights": [],
  "text": "example"
}
```

  },
  "usage": {}
}

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

## UsageUpdated

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageUpdated.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: If usageScope is provider_session or model_session, counterKey is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| usageScope | no | ["agent","provider_session","model_session"] |  |
| counterKey | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| contextTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| contextSessionId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| reasoningTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cacheWriteTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cacheWrite1hTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1} |
| accountId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| counterMode | no | ["cumulative","incremental"] |  |
| type | yes | `"usage.updated"` |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| inputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cachedInputTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| contextWindow | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| costUsd | no | number | {"minimum":0} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "billingMode": "subscription",
  "cacheWrite1hTokens": 8,
  "cacheWriteTokens": 1,
  "counterMode": "cumulative",
  "inputTokens": 2,
  "outputTokens": 0,
  "type": "usage.updated"
}
```
